Given current h, sum target exact. For pair (p,q), define x = h_p + t, y = h_q - t (or for arbitrary direction keep sum). t ∈ [min(room), max]. Need minimize max over lags affected by p or q. There is a cross term if both p and q appear in same product in c_m. That yields B t^2. B can be - coefficient if product p,q appears. Number of such lags at most 2? a_m = ∑_i α_i[m] v_i. But α_i[m] = interval indicator - h_{i+t} - h_{i-t}. We can derive a_m directly:
c_m = ∑_{j in I_m} h_j - ∑_{j in I_m} h_j h_{j+t}.
Δ linear along v: a_m = ∑_{j in I_m} v_j - ∑_{j in I_m} (v_j h_{j+t} + h_j v_{j+t}). This can be computed by convolutions/correlations:
Term1 s_m = sum of v over valid interval (like s for h but v). Term2 bilinear = ∑_{j} v_j h_{j+t} + h_j v_{j+t} (where valid both?) For each lag t, both valid. This is correlation of v with h and h with v at lag t plus maybe endpoints. Specifically a_m = s_m - (corr_v_h[t] + corr_h_v[t]) where corr_v_h[t] = ∑_{j valid} v_j h_{j+t}, corr_h_v[t] = ∑ h_j v_{j+t}. But since valid same, corr_h_v[t] = corr_v_h[-t]? For length, yes.
b_m = -∑ v_j v_{j+t} = -corr_v_v[t] (with valid). For t=0, corr_v_v[0]=∑v_j^2, b=-∑v^2.
Thus a,b can be computed via FFT convolutions of sequences v,h. For direction dense, O(n log n). Good!

```
def active_linear_weights(w,n):
  off=n-1
  p0=w[off]
  pos=w[off+1:]
  neg=w[:off][::-1]
  pos_pref=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(pos)))
  neg_pref=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(neg)))
  idx=np.arange(n)
  return p0 + pos_pref[n-1-idx] + neg_pref[idx]
```

Given weights p (softmax active), we want minimize a quadratic proxy Q_p(h)=p^T c(h). This is nonconvex but can be minimized by coordinate descent extremely fast because Q is a weighted sum of c; derivative wrt each coordinate is linear in h. One-coordinate minimization for Q (not max) is easy: Q as function of h_p is quadratic with coefficient B_p = -∑_t p_t I(p pairs at t) (negative/zero? includes - for t=0: -p0*? h_p^2, product offdiag: moving one variable changes products linearly? Since Q is quadratic, univariate is quadratic with coefficient maybe -p0 h? Actually Q has -h_i^2 diagonal and -h_ih_j offdiag; fixing others, coefficient of h_p^2 is -p0; plus linear terms. Minimizing a concave quadratic (-a x^2 + bx) over [0,1] with equality? Minimum at boundaries, so Q_p optimized favors binary. But max objective wants keep all c low; weighted proxy maybe pushes binary. Could alternate: p=softmax c, set h=argmin Q_p (or projected coordinate descent), update c. This is like von Neumann minimax, maybe find global? For fixed p, Q nonconvex, but convex? Q = linear - quadratic with offdiag negative. The Hessian has offdiag negative; not convex. But univariate minimum at boundaries. Global argmin of a quadratic with indefinite? If diagonal negative (for p0) and offdiag maybe, could be NP? But one-dimensional CD can converge to local.

```
  # for d where p-d>=0: a[off-d] += 1 - h[p-d]

```

Positive lag d (t=+d):
- if p+d < n: a[off+d] += 1 - h[p+d]
- if p-d >=0: a[off+d] -= h[p-d]
Negative lag -d:
- if p-d >=0: a[off-d] += 1 - h[p-d]
- if p+d < n: a[off-d] -= h[p+d]

Let's implement pair_step and use it in a limited loop:
```
def pair_pass(h,n,max_pairs,deadline,rng,select_alpha=5000):
  best=...
  for it in range(max_pairs):
    if time>deadline break
    # compute grad occasionally? c changes; compute grad every 10 pairs
    if it%10==0:
      _,g = smooth_value_grad(h,n,select_alpha)
      neg=np.where(g < -1e-5)[0]; pos=np.where(g > 1e-5)[0]
    if len(neg) or len(pos): p=neg[rng], q=pos[rng]
    else: p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
    if p==q continue
    s=pair_step(c,h,p,q,n) # also updates c? We can have it return s if accept
```

Implementation:
```
def pair_step(c,h,p,q,n):
  if p==q: return c.max()
  dp=abs(p-q); off=n-1; L=2*n-1
  ap=deriv_one(h,p,n); aq=deriv_one(h,q,n); a=ap-aq
  b0=c.max()
  lo=max(-h[p], h[q]-1.0); hi=min(1-h[p], h[q])
  if hi <= lo + 1e-13: return c
  m1=off+dp; m2=off-dp
  # define feasibility(c, target,L): returns bool and maybe bounds? For bisection just bool
  for _ in range(50):
    mid=(loL+hiL)/2
    # linear constraints all m except m1,m2; use vector masks precomputed? cross positions small.
```

`project` code:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target = n/2.0
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
    s = h.sum()
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-13: return h
    if s > target:
        lo, hi = 0.0, 1.0
    else:
        lo, hi = -1.0, 0.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       x=np.clip(h-mid,0,1)
       sx=x.sum()
       if sx < target: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    x=np.clip(h-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    diff=target-x.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
       idx = np.argmax(1.0-x) if diff>0 else np.argmax(x)
       x[idx]+=diff
       x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    return x
```
If s > target, need subtract (τ positive). At τ=1, x all 0 sum 0. If s<target, τ negative; at τ=-1, x all 1 sum n. Good.
If h already clipped and sum target, return. If after final adjust diff and clip maybe slight. Fine.

`compute_c` simple: `np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')`. Note for h float, 1-h.

`smooth_value_grad` used for pair selection and L-BFGS. Need efficient and stable. We can return f and grad or just grad.
Implementation details:
```
def smooth_eval(h, n, alpha, need_grad):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    m = c.max()
    sh = alpha*(c-m)
    ms = sh.max()
    e = np.exp(sh-ms)
    se = e.sum()
    val = (m + (ms + np.log(se))/alpha) * (2.0/n)
    if not need_grad: return val, c, e/se
    off=n-1
    w = e/se
    s_ws = w + w[::-1]
    H = cross_corr(h, s_ws, n)
    A = active_linear(w,n)
    g = (2.0/n)*(A-H)
    return val, g
```
`cross_corr` input h length n, s_ws length 2n-1. Use `np.fft.rfft`. Need N: L = len(s); conv_len = n + L - 1 = 3n-2. `N = 1 << (conv_len - 1).bit_length()`. If n=0 no. For conv_len maybe power of two exactly? If conv_len=1? n=1: L=1, conv_len=1, bit_length 0? `1 << (0).bit_length()` =1. But n large. Use `N=1; while N<conv_len: N<<=1` to avoid bit_length? Top-level helper; but while inside okay. `N = 1 << (conv_len - 1).bit_length()` works if conv_len-1 >0; for conv_len=1, bit_length 0 ->1. Good.
After irfft, return `out[off:off+n]`. Could have small imaginary due to numerical; ignore.
Potential issue: `np.fft.rfft` default n=N, zero padding. Good.

Can we avoid FFT for small n? Not needed.

`pen_obj`:
```
def pen_obj(x, n, alpha, lam, beta):
    h = np.clip(x,0,1)
    val,_ = smooth_eval(h,n,alpha,False)
    v = h.sum()-n/2
    val += 0.5*lam*v*v
    if beta:
       s = h*(1-h)
       val += beta*np.sum(s*s)
    return val
```
`pen_grad` similar with need_grad True, grad += lam*v*ones; if beta grad += 2*beta*s*(1-2*h).
But smooth_eval computes val and grad. For L-BFGS, gradient must be consistent with objective when x within [0,1]. If h clipped same. Good.
Potential issue: smooth_eval returns grad of smooth objective not including dx? yes includes dx. Penalty scale lam. If lam=1e5 and beta small, grad fine.
`smoothing_objective` uses `alpha` maybe integer. Use float.

`exact_cd` fixed:
```
def exact_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng,stop_tol=1e-13):
  h=project_feasible(h,n); c=np.correlate(...); best_c5=c.max()*2/n
  for it in range(max_passes):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    improved=False
    order=list(range(n)); if rng and it%2: rng.shuffle(order)
    for p in order:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      h0=h[p]
      if h0 <=1e-12 or h0 >=1-1e-12: continue
      xn=solve_1d(c,h,p,n)
      if abs(xn-h0)>1e-11:
         if xn < 0 or xn >1: continue
         update_corr(c,h,p,h0,xn,n)
         h[p]=xn
         improved=True
    if not improved: break
    h_new=project_feasible(h,n)
    if np.max(np.abs(h_new-h)) > 1e-13:
       h=h_new
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h,full)
    else:
       # If sum off? project would change; if not, okay
    # update best? c is current; but projection maybe changed c (if recomputed). compute current max.
    c5cur=c.max()*2/n
    if c5cur < best_c5: best_c5=c5cur
  h=project_feasible(h,n); c=...; return h,c.max()*2/n
```
Need if projection changes h but c not recomputed? We recompute whenever h_new differs. Good.
If projection changes h by tiny but c should differ, threshold 1e-13 may miss; but tiny effect negligible. Could recompute if any diff >0? Expensive. Use 1e-12.
`max_passes` parameter: exact_cd can be called with 200; but each pass O(n). We can pass dynamically.
Maybe coordinate updates can get stuck in cycles due projection/mismatch; if after pass c5 increased, still accept? Since we maintain local c, maybe. We can track best_h/c and restore if c5 increases beyond? In exact_cd, coordinate updates are minimax so monotonically non-increasing ignoring projection. Projection can increase. We can save best_c5 and best_h before pass; if after pass c5cur > best_c5 + 1e-9, restore? But h/c changed; restoring may prevent progress if projection increased but next pass reduces. However to be safe, keep best snapshot. At end return best. Implement:
```
best_h=h.copy(); best_c5=...
after pass/projection, if c5cur < best_c5: best_h=h.copy(); best_c5=...
```

Pair_pass implementation and integration. We need maintain c consistent with h. It doesn't project. It updates c initially from h. It can be called with h feasible.
```
def pair_polish(h,n,max_pairs,deadline,rng,alpha=5000):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); c=corr; best=h.copy(); best_c=c.max()
   g=None
   for it in range(max_pairs):
      if time>deadline: break
      if g is None or it%20==0:
         _,g = smooth_eval(h,n,alpha,need_grad=True)
         # maybe threshold
         # Use h ranks if g not informative
         neg = np.where(g < -1e-7)[0]
         pos = np.where(g > 1e-7)[0]
         # If few, use h
         if len(neg) < 10: neg = np.argpartition(h,10)[:max(1,10)]? For increase choose low h.
      if len(neg) and len(pos): p = neg[rng.randint(len(neg))]; q = pos[rng.randint(len(pos))]; 
      elif len(pos)>0 and h[p?].
      else:
         low = np.argpartition(h, 20)[:20]; high = np.argpartition(h, n-21)[-20:]; p=low[rng...], q=high[rng...]
      if p==q: continue
      oldmax = c.max()
      newmax = pair_step(c,h,p,q,n)
      if newmax < best_c - 1e-12:
         best_c=newmax; best_h=h.copy()
      # even if equal, continue; h/c mutated if accepted (pair_step accepted if newmax <= old+tol). But pair_step could accept equal; okay.
   # final feasible
   h=project_feasible(h,n); c=corr
   if c.max()<best_c: best...
   return best_h,best_c*2/n
```

```
def deriv_one(h,p,n):
   off=n-1; a=np.zeros(2*n-1)
   a[off]=1.0-2.0*h[p]
   d=np.arange(1,n)
   pos = p+d < n
   neg = p-d >= 0
   if any(pos):
     idx_pos = p+d[pos]
     a[off+d[pos]] += 1.0 - h[idx_pos]
     a[off-d[pos]] -= h[idx_pos]
   if any(neg):
     idx_neg = p-d[neg]
     a[off+d[neg]] -= h[idx_neg]
     a[off-d[neg]] += 1.0 - h[idx_neg]
   return a
```
Need `d[pos]` arrays; if no pos, skip. `a[off-d[pos]]` indices positive? off-d >=1 for d<=off. Good.

`pair_step(c,h,p,q,n)`:
```
def pair_step(c,h,p,q,n):
    if p==q: return float(c.max())
    off=n-1; L=2*n-1
    a = deriv_one(h,p,n) - deriv_one(h,q,n)
    old_max=float(c.max())
    lo_s=max(-h[p], h[q]-1.0); hi_s=min(1.0-h[p], h[q])
    if hi_s <= lo_s + 1e-14: return old_max
    d=abs(p-q); m1=off+d; m2=off-d
    # lin indices all but m1,m2
    lin = np.ones(L,bool); lin[m1]=lin[m2]=False
    al=a[lin]; cl=c[lin]
    pos_mask=al>1e-12; neg_mask=al<-1e-12; zero_mask=~(pos_mask|neg_mask)
    Llo=0.0; Lhi=old_max
    eps=1e-12
    if not zero_mask.any() and not pos_mask.any() and not neg_mask.any(): lin_min=0? (not possible)
    for _ in range(45):
       mid=(Llo+Lhi)/2
       s_lo=lo_s; s_hi=hi_s
       feas=True
       if zero_mask.any() and cl[zero_mask].max() > mid + 1e-10: feas=False
       if feas and pos_mask.any():
          s_hi=min(s_hi, float(np.min((mid-cl[pos_mask])/al[pos_mask])))
       if feas and neg_mask.any():
          s_lo=max(s_lo, float(np.max((mid-cl[neg_mask])/al[neg_mask])))
       if s_lo > s_hi + 1e-11: feas=False
       if feas:
          # cross m1
          for m in (m1,m2):
             A=a[m]; C=c[m]-mid
             # s^2 + A s + C <=0
             disc=A*A-4.0*C
             if disc < -1e-11:
                feas=False; break
             if disc < 0.0: disc=0.0
             rt=np.sqrt(disc)
             r1=(-A-rt)/2.0; r2=(-A+rt)/2.0
             s_lo=max(s_lo,r1); s_hi=min(s_hi,r2)
          if s_lo > s_hi + 1e-11: feas=False
       if feas: Lhi=mid
       else: Llo=mid
    L=Lhi
    # compute s interval at L
    s_lo=lo_s; s_hi=hi_s
    if pos_mask.any(): s_hi=min(...)
    if neg_mask.any(): s_lo=max(...)
    if zero_mask.any() and cl[zero_mask].max() > L+1e-10: s_lo,s_hi=1,0
    if s_lo <= s_hi:
       for m in (m1,m2): ... roots using L; clip
    else: return old_max
    if s_lo > s_hi: return old_max
    # choose median or interior? choose midpoint to be safely inside (roots inclusive). Midpoint maybe.
    s=(s_lo+s_hi)/2
    # Ensure bounds
    s=min(max(s,lo_s),hi_s)
    if abs(s)<1e-14: s=0.0; return old_max? If s=0 no change.
    # Update
    backup=c.copy()
    c += a*s
    c[m1] += s*s
    c[m2] += s*s
    h[p] += s; h[q] -= s
    # clip tiny? due float
    h[p]=min(max(h[p],0.0),1.0); h[q]=min(max(h[q],0.0),1.0)
    new_max=float(c.max())
    if new_max > old_max + 1e-9:
       c[:] = backup; h[p]-=s; h[q]+=s; return old_max
    return new_max
```
Zero_mask: `cl[zero_mask]` maybe uninitialized? cl exists. If no zero skip. `s_lo` and `s_hi` with linear at final if infeasible set invalid.
For cross roots, if disc==0 one point. Intersection.

Since s=0 is always feasible at L=old_max but not necessarily at L* if L*<old_max. At L*, 0 may be infeasible if it violates (meaning old max affected). The feasible interval excludes 0. That's necessary to reduce. Midpoint okay. But if L* close old_max and interval wide, midpoint may cause big oscillation yet max not exceed L*. Could still be okay. Could choose smallest |s| in interval to minimize change: if 0 in interval, choose 0 (but then no improvement? if L*<oldmax, 0 shouldn't be feasible; if due tolerance, no move). If 0 not in interval, choose boundary closest to 0. This reduces change while achieving L. Let's do:

```
else: s=s_hi
```

```
def pair_polish(h,n,max_pairs,deadline,rng):
  h=project_feasible(h,n).copy(); c=compute_c; best_c=c.max(); best_h=h.copy()
  g=None

    if time.time()>deadline: break
    if g is None or it%20==0:
       _,g=smooth_eval(h,n,5000.0,True)
       neg=np.where(g < -1e-6)[0]; pos=np.where(g > 1e-6)[0]

       high=np.argpartition(h,n-k-1)[-k:] maybe if k=n use all
       p=low[rng.randint(k)]; q=high[rng.randint(k)]
    if p==q: continue
    oldmax=float(c.max())
    newmax=pair_step(c,h,p,q,n)
    # if newmax < best_c - 1e-11: update; no_improve=0 else no_improve++
    if newmax < best_c - 1e-11:
      best_c=newmax; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
    else:
      no_improve+=1
      if no_improve > 2000: break

  if c.max() < best_c: best...
  return best_h, best_c*(2/n)
```

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5; rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  try: h=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n=len(h)
  except: h=np.full(512,0.5); n=512
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5
  # Phase 1 exact_cd on parent
  if time < deadline-120:
     h1,c1=exact_cd(h,n,80,deadline,rng)
     if c1<best_c: best=...
  # Phase 2 pair polish
  if time < deadline-120:
     h2,c2=pair_polish(best_h,n,2000,deadline,rng)
     if c2<best_c: best=...
  # Phase 3 L-BFGS smooth
  if time < deadline-100:
     h3,c3=lbfgs_refine(best_h,n,['...'],deadline,rng)
     if c3<best_c: best=...
  # Phase 4 exact_cd again
  if time < deadline-60: exact_cd(best_h,n,100)
  # Phase 5 multi-res
  for n_try in [800,650,600,550,500,450,400,350]:
     if time > deadline-80: break
     h_int=interp_h(best_h,len(best_h),n_try)
     h_o,c_o=exact_cd(h_int,n_try,40,deadline,rng)
     if c_o<best_c-1e-9: best=...
     if time < deadline-50:
        h_o2,c_o2=pair_polish(h_o,n_try,300,deadline,rng)
        if c_o2<best_c-1e-9: best=...
  # Phase 6 random perturbations on best n
  n=len(best_h)
  for scale in [0.03,0.02,0.01,0.005]:
     if time > deadline-40: break
     hp=project_feasible(best_h+rng.normal(0,scale,n),n)
     ho,co=exact_cd(hp,n,50,deadline,rng)
     if co<best_c-1e-9: best=...
     if time < deadline-30: po,pc=pair_polish(ho,n,400,deadline,rng); update
  # final long exact_cd + pair
  ho,co=exact_cd(best_h,n,60,deadline,rng); update
  if time < deadline-10: pair_polish(best_h,n,1000,deadline,rng); update
  best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n); c=... best_c=...
  return best_h,best_c,n
```

Now, let's scrutinize the mathematical gradient for smooth_eval. A formula using intervals: For a lag t (positive d), linear sum s_t = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1-d} h_j. Derivative A_d for coordinate k: p_d if k ≤ n-1-d. For negative t=-d, s=∑_{j=d}^{n-1} h_j. Derivative p_-d if k ≥ d. Diagonal p0 all k. Our `A = p0 + pos_pref[n-1-k] + neg_pref[k]`. For k=n-1, pos_pref[0]=0, neg_pref[n-1]=sum p_-d for d=1..n-1, so A=p0 + all negative. Intervals: k last gets diagonal and all negative lags (for -d interval starts d <= last yes) but not positive lags except? For positive d, interval 0..n-1-d excludes last, yes. Good. For k=0, pos_pref[n-1]=sum all positive. Good.
Derivative of c_m wrt h_k for positive part is 1. But we previously combined A-H where H includes bilinear derivative. H with s_ws = p_d + p_-d. def exact_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng):
```
  h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
  c=compute_c(h,n)
  best_c=c.max()*2/n; best_h=h.copy()
  for it in range(max_passes):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    improved=False
    # order
    if rng is not None:
       try:
          _,g=smooth_eval(h,n,2000.0,True)
          order=list(np.argsort(-np.abs(g)))
       except Exception:
          order=list(range(n)); rng.shuffle(order)
    else: order=list(range(n))
    for p in order:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      h0=h[p]
      if h0<=1e-12 or h0>=1-1e-12: continue
      xn=solve_1d(c,h,p,n)
      if abs(xn-h0)>1e-11:
        update_corr(c,h,p,h0,xn,n); h[p]=xn; improved=True
    if not improved:
       # still project? if h unchanged feasible; break
       break
    h_new=project_feasible(h,n)
    if np.any(np.abs(h_new-h)>1e-14):
       h=h_new; c=compute_c(h,n)
    else:
       h=h_new  # sum maybe tiny
    c5cur=c.max()*2/n
    if c5cur < best_c - 1e-15:
       best_c=c5cur; best_h=h.copy()
    # if not improved, continue? maybe current h feasible but no new best; continue next pass? Could if projection altered h, changes may now allow more.
  if time.time()<deadline:
    # project left h and if improved
  return best_h,best_c
```

Detailed solve_1d code:
```
def solve_1d(c,h,p,n):
    off=n-1
    m0=off-p; m1=2*n-2-p
    ms=np.arange(m0,m1+1)
    jf=p+ms-off; jb=p-ms+off
    vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    a=np.zeros(len(ms))
    if vf.any(): a[vf]+=1.0-h[jf[vf]]
    if vb.any(): a[vb]-=h[jb[vb]]
    cb=c[ms].copy()
    di=np.where(ms==off)[0]
    has=0
    if di.size:
       has=1; a[di[0]]=0.0; cdiag=cb[di[0]]
    unaff=0.0
    if m0>0: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[:m0])))
    if m1<2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[m1+1:])))
    Llo=max(0.0,unaff); Lhi=float(np.max(c))
    neg=a<-1e-13; pos=a>1e-13; zero=~(neg|pos)
    eps=1e-12
    def? no closures. Inline feasibility loop.
    for _ in range(42):
       L=(Llo+Lhi)*0.5
       xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0; ok=True
       if ok and zero.any() and float(np.max(cb[zero])) > L+1e-11: ok=False
       if ok and pos.any(): xhi=min(xhi,float(np.min((L-cb[pos])/a[pos])))
       if ok and neg.any(): xlo=max(xlo,float(np.max((L-cb[neg])/a[neg])))
       xlo=max(xlo,0.0); xhi=min(xhi,1.0)
       if ok and xlo>xhi+1e-11: ok=False
       if ok and has:
          Cq=L-cdiag+h0-h0*h0
          if Cq < -1e-10: ok=False
          elif Cq <= 0.25+1e-10:
             if Cq < 0.0: Cq=0.0
             disc=max(0.0,1.0-4.0*Cq)
             r1=(1.0-np.sqrt(disc))*0.5; r2=(1.0+np.sqrt(disc))*0.5
             if xlo > r1+eps and xhi < r2-eps: ok=False
       if ok: Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    ... choose x
```
Need `h0=h[p]` before.
If Cq >0.25+1e-10 all feasible; if Cq between 0.25-eps and 0.25, disc maybe small. The `elif Cq <=0.25+eps` includes; if Cq>0.25, disc negative -> sqrt0, r1=r2=0.5; condition xlo>0.5 and xhi<0.5 would infeasible? But if Cq>0.25 all feasible. So need separate:
```
if Cq < -eps: ok=False
elif Cq < 0.25-eps:
   # compute roots
   if Cq <0: Cq=0
   ... infeas if inside
# else all feasible
```

```
if not ok: return h0
segs=[]
if has:
  Cq=...
  if Cq < -eps: return h0
  seg_all=[xlo,xhi]
  if Cq < 0.25-eps:
     Cq=max(0,Cq); disc...; r1,r2

     l=max(xlo,r2); r=min(xhi,1); if l<=r+eps: segs.append((l,r))

else segs.append((xlo,xhi))
```

```
def coord_info(h,p,n):
    off=n-1
    ms=np.arange(off-p, 2*n-2-p+1)
    jf=p+ms-off; jb=p-ms+off
    a=np.zeros(len(ms))
    vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n)
    if vf.any(): a[vf]+=1.0-h[jf[vf]]
    vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    if vb.any(): a[vb]-=h[jb[vb]]
    return ms,a
```
Length n. Good.

```
def deriv_one(h,p,n):
    full=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    ms,a=coord_info(h,p,n)
```

Now, pair_polish uses `pair_step` which may update. It needs `c` initialized. It can use `c5_counts = c.max()`.
```
def pair_polish(h,n,max_pairs,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy(); c=compute_c(h)
    best_c=c.max(); best_h=h.copy(); no=0
    k=min(80,n)
    for _ in range(max_pairs):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if rng.random() < 0.15:
           p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
       else:
           low=np.argpartition(h,k-1)[:k]
           high=np.argpartition(h,n-k-1)[-k:] if k<n else np.arange(n)
           i=int(rng.randint(k)); j=int(rng.randint(k))
           p=int(low[i]); q=int(high[j])
       if p==q: continue
       old=float(c.max())
       new=pair_step(c,h,p,q,n)
       if new < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=new; best_h=h.copy(); no=0
       else:
          no += 1
          if no > 1200: break
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c=compute_c(h)
    cur=c.max()
    if cur < best_c: best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c*2.0/n
```
Computing argpartition every pair expensive (O(n) sort) if 3000 -> 2M, okay but maybe. We can recompute low/high every 50 pairs and keep arrays. Better:
```
low=high=None; cnt=0
each loop if cnt%30==0: compute low/high.
```
Implement to save.
If n<80, k=n; high all; p/q could same. If n=1, pair skip. n>1.
Use `rng.randint(k)` returns np int; cast.

`compute_c(h) = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')`; top-level no n needed.

`interp_h`:
```
def interp_h(h,n0,n1):
   if n0==n1: return h.copy()
   xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n0,endpoint=False)
   xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n1,endpoint=False)
   return project_feasible(np.interp(xn,xo,h),n1)
```
If h features at boundaries with endpoint false, okay.

Let's write `project_feasible` carefully with `np.sum` repeatedly. 80 iterations * n; okay. Could reduce 60.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    targ=float(n)/2.0
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0,1)
    s=float(h.sum())
    if abs(s-targ)<1e-14:
        return h
    if s>targ:
        lo,hi=0.0,1.0
    else:
        lo,hi=-1.0,0.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        x=np.clip(h-mid,0,1)
        sx=float(x.sum())
        if sx<targ:
            hi=mid
        else:
            lo=mid
    x=np.clip(h-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    d=targ-float(x.sum())
    if d!=0.0:
        if d>0: idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-x))
        else: idx=int(np.argmax(x))
        x[idx]=float(np.clip(x[idx]+d,0,1))
    return x
```
If s target but values outside, returns clipped maybe sum off? Then d adjust. Good.

`coord_info`:
```
def coord_info(h,p,n):
    off=n-1
    ms=np.arange(off-p, 2*n-2-p+1, dtype=int)
    jf=p+ms-off
    jb=p-ms+off
    vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n)
    a=np.zeros(len(ms))
    if vf.any(): a[vf]+=1.0-h[jf[vf]]
    vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    if vb.any(): a[vb]-=h[jb[vb]]
    return ms,a
```
For n maybe 1, m0=0,m1=0, jf/jb arrays length1; works.

`update_corr`:
```
def update_corr(c,h,p,old,neu,n):
    delta=neu-old
    if abs(delta)<1e-14: return
    ms,a=coord_info(h,p,n)
    c[ms]+=delta*a
    off=n-1
    di=np.where(ms==off)[0]
    if di.size: c[ms[di[0]]]-=delta*delta
```
Here `h` must be original before update. In exact_cd, call `update_corr(c,h,p,h0,xn,n)` before h[p]=xn.

`exact_cd`:
```
def exact_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c=compute_c(h)
    best_c=2.0*float(c.max())/n
    best_h=h.copy()
    for it in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if it%2==0:
          order=list(np.argsort(np.abs(h-0.5)))
       else:
          order=list(range(n)); rng.shuffle(order)
       improved=False
       for p in order:
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          h0=float(h[p])
          if h0<=1e-12 or h0>=1.0-1e-12: continue
          xn=solve_1d(c,h,p,n)
          if abs(xn-h0)>1e-11:
             update_corr(c,h,p,h0,xn,n)
             h[p]=xn
             improved=True
       if not improved: break
       h_new=project_feasible(h,n)
       if np.max(np.abs(h_new-h)) > 1e-13:
          h=h_new; c=compute_c(h)
       else:
          h=h_new
       cur=2.0*float(c.max())/n
       if cur < best_c - 1e-13:
          best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c(h)
    cur=2.0*float(c.max())/n
    if cur < best_c - 1e-13: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
    return best_h,best_c
```
If `np.max` on h_new-h where n=0? no.
`order=list(np.argsort(...))` uses current h; if many binary, argsort stable. Good.
If `Improved` false but projection might have changed? no.

`deriv_one` as:
```
def deriv_one(h,p,n):
   full=np.zeros(2*n-1)
   ms,a=coord_info(h,p,n)
   full[ms]=a
   return full
```

`pair_step`:
```
def pair_step(c,h,p,q,n):
    if p==q: return float(c.max())
    off=n-1
    a=deriv_one(h,p,n)-deriv_one(h,q,n)
    old=float(c.max())
    if old <= 1e-14: return old
    lo=float(max(-h[p], h[q]-1.0))
    hi=float(min(1.0-h[p], h[q]))
    if hi <= lo + 1e-13: return old
    d=abs(p-q)
    m1=off+d; m2=off-d
    lin=np.ones(2*n-1, dtype=bool); lin[m1]=False; lin[m2]=False
    al=a[lin]; cl=c[lin]
    pos=al>1e-12; neg=al<-1e-12
    pac=al[pos]; pcb=cl[pos]; nac=al[neg]; ncb=cl[neg]
    zero= ~(pos|neg)
    zmax=float(np.max(cl[zero])) if zero.any() else -1.0
    Llo=0.0; Lhi=old
    for _ in range(42):
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       s_lo=lo; s_hi=hi; ok=True
       if zmax > L + 1e-10: ok=False
       if ok and pac.size:
          s_hi=min(s_hi, float(np.min((L-pcb)/pac)))
       if ok and nac.size:
          s_lo=max(s_lo, float(np.max((L-ncb)/nac)))
       s_lo=max(s_lo,lo); s_hi=min(s_hi,hi)
       if s_lo > s_hi + 1e-11: ok=False
       if ok:
          A1=a[m1]; C1=c[m1]-L
          disc=A1*A1-4.0*C1
          if disc < -1e-10: ok=False
          else:
             rt=np.sqrt(max(0.0,disc)); r1=(-A1-rt)*0.5; r2=(-A1+rt)*0.5
             s_lo=max(s_lo,r1); s_hi=min(s_hi,r2)
             if s_lo > s_hi + 1e-11: ok=False
       if ok:
          A2=a[m2]; C2=c[m2]-L
          disc=A2*A2-4.0*C2
          if disc < -1e-10: ok=False
          else:
             rt=np.sqrt(max(0.0,disc)); r1=(-A2-rt)*0.5; r2=(-A2+rt)*0.5
             s_lo=max(s_lo,r1); s_hi=min(s_hi,r2)
             if s_lo > s_hi + 1e-11: ok=False
       if ok: Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    s_lo=lo; s_hi=hi
    ok=True
    if zmax > L + 1e-9: ok=False
    if ok and pac.size: s_hi=min(s_hi,float(np.min((L-pcb)/pac)))
    if ok and nac.size: s_lo=max(s_lo,float(np.max((L-ncb)/nac)))
    s_lo=max(s_lo,lo); s_hi=min(s_hi,hi)
    if s_lo > s_hi + 1e-9: return old
    for m in (m1,m2):
       A=a[m]; C=c[m]-L
       disc=A*A-4.0*C
       if disc < -1e-8: return old
       rt=np.sqrt(max(0.0,disc)); r1=(-A-rt)*0.5; r2=(-A+rt)*0.5
       s_lo=max(s_lo,r1); s_hi=min(s_hi,r2)
    if s_lo > s_hi + 1e-9: return old
    if s_lo <= 0.0 <= s_hi:
       s=0.0
    elif 0.0 < s_lo:
       s=s_lo
    else:
       s=s_hi
    if abs(s) < 1e-13: return old
    backup=c.copy()
    c[:] = c + a*s  # c += a*s
    c[m1] += s*s
    c[m2] += s*s
    h[p] = float(h[p] + s)
    h[q] = float(h[q] - s)
    new=float(c.max())
    if new > old + 1e-9:
       c[:] = backup
       h[p] -= s
       h[q] += s
       return old
    return new
```
Need be careful restore: after setting h[p]=h[p]+s, restore by `h[p] -= s`, `h[q] += s` returns original. Good. But if we do `c[:] = c + a*s` creates new then assign; okay. Use `c += a*s` but then backup restore. `c += a*s` in place; then cross. Restore `c[:]=backup`. Good.

```
s = min(max(s, lo+1e-12), hi-1e-12) if hi>lo+2e-12 else 0
```


`pair_polish` with selection arrays. Need ensure if n=1, k=1, high all. p==q always? if p=random 0 q=random 0, skip. no. Pair step not.
```
def pair_polish(h,n,max_pairs,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy(); c=compute_c(h)
    best_c=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy()
    no=0
    k=min(80,n)
    low=np.arange(n); high=low
    for it in range(max_pairs):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if it%25==0 or k==n? maybe compute every 25:
          low=np.argpartition(h,k-1)[:k]
          high=np.argpartition(h,n-k-1)[-k:] if k<n else np.arange(n)
       if rng.random()<0.15:
          p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
       else:
          p=int(low[int(rng.randint(k))]); q=int(high[int(rng.randint(k))])
       if p==q or n<2: continue
       new=pair_step(c,h,p,q,n)
       if new < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=new; best_h=h.copy(); no=0
       else:
          no+=1
          if no>1500: break
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c=compute_c(h)
    cur=float(c.max())
    if cur < best_c: best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h, 2.0*best_c/n
```
If `n-k-1` negative when k=n, handled. If k=0? n>0 k>=1.

`run` with caps. Need ensure if `initial_h_values` length 0? fallback.
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    overall=t0+max(10.0,float(budget_s)-5.0)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
       h=np.full(700,0.5)
    n=int(len(h))
```

Let's refine solve_1d code compact:
```
def solve_1d(c,h,p,n):
    off=n-1; h0=float(h[p]); ms,a=coord_info(h,p,n); cb=c[ms].copy()
    di=np.where(ms==off)[0]; has=bool(di.size)
    if has:
        a[di[0]]=0.0; cdiag=cb[di[0]]
    m0=int(ms[0]); m1=int(ms[-1]); last=2*n-2
    unaff=0.0
    if m0>0: unaff=float(np.max(c[:m0]))
    if m1<last: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[m1+1:])))
    valid=np.ones(len(ms),bool)
    if has: valid[di[0]]=False
    neg=valid&(a<-1e-13); pos=valid&(a>1e-13); zero=valid&~neg&~pos
    pac=a[pos]; pcb=cb[pos]; nac=a[neg]; ncb=cb[neg]
    zmax=float(np.max(cb[zero])) if zero.any() else -1e30
    Llo=max(0.0,unaff); Lhi=float(c.max());
    for _ in range(42):
        L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi); xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0; ok=True
        if zmax>L+1e-10: ok=False
        if ok and pac.size: xhi=min(xhi,float(np.min((L-pcb)/pac)))
        if ok and nac.size: xlo=max(xlo,float(np.max((L-ncb)/nac)))
        xlo=max(xlo,0.0); xhi=min(xhi,1.0)
        if xlo>xhi+1e-11: ok=False
        if ok and has:
            Cq=L-cdiag+h0-h0*h0
            if Cq<-1e-10: ok=False
            elif Cq<0.25-1e-10:
                Cq=max(0.0,Cq); disc=max(0.0,1.0-4.0*Cq); sq=np.sqrt(disc)
                r1=(1.0-sq)*0.5; r2=(1.0+sq)*0.5
                if xlo>r1+1e-10 and xhi<r2-1e-10: ok=False
        if ok: Lhi=L
        else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi; xlo=0.0; xhi=1.0; ok=True
    if zmax>L+1e-9: ok=False
    if ok and pac.size: xhi=min(xhi,float(np.min((L-pcb)/pac)))
    if ok and nac.size: xlo=max(xlo,float(np.max((L-ncb)/nac)))
    xlo=max(xlo,0.0); xhi=min(xhi,1.0)
    if not ok or xlo>xhi+1e-9: return h0
    segs=[]
    if has:
        Cq=L-cdiag+h0-h0*h0
        if Cq<-1e-9: return h0
        if Cq<0.25-1e-9:
            Cq=max(0.0,Cq); disc=max(0.0,1.0-4.0*Cq); sq=np.sqrt(disc)
            r1=(1.0-sq)*0.5; r2=(1.0+sq)*0.5
            l=max(xlo,0.0); r=min(xhi,r1)
            if l<=r+1e-9: segs.append((l,r))
            l=max(xlo,r2); r=min(xhi,1.0)
            if l<=r+1e-9: segs.append((l,r))
        else: segs.append((xlo,xhi))
    else: segs.append((xlo,xhi))
    bestx=h0; bestd=1e100
    for l,r in segs:
        if h0<=l: d=l-h0; x=l
        elif h0>=r: d=h0-r; x=r
        else: d=0.0; x=h0
        if d<bestd: bestd=d; bestx=x
    return float(np.clip(bestx,0.0,1.0))
```
Looks okay. If `zero.any()` else zmax -1e30. If there are no valid lags (n=0) not.
If `pac.size` but `pac` all positive; `(L-pcb)/pac` could include values >1 and <0; min/max works.
In bisection, if `zmax > L` but diagonal can reduce? diag excluded. Good.

`pair_step` compact:
```
def pair_step(c,h,p,q,n):
    if p==q: return float(c.max())
    a=deriv_one(h,p,n)-deriv_one(h,q,n)
    old=float(c.max())
    if old<=1e-14: return old
    lo=max(-h[p],h[q]-1.0); hi=min(1.0-h[p],h[q])
    if hi<=lo+1e-13: return old
    off=n-1; m1=off+abs(p-q); m2=off-abs(p-q)
    lin=np.ones(2*n-1,bool); lin[m1]=lin[m2]=False
    al=a[lin]; cl=c[lin]
    pos=al>1e-12; neg=al<-1e-12
    pac=al[pos]; pcb=cl[pos]; nac=al[neg]; ncb=cl[neg]
    zero=~pos&~neg
    zmax=float(cl[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e30
    Llo=0.0; Lhi=old
    for _ in range(42):
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi); slo=lo; shi=hi; ok=True
       if zmax>L+1e-10: ok=False
       if ok and pac.size: shi=min(shi,float(np.min((L-pcb)/pac)))
       if ok and nac.size: slo=max(slo,float(np.max((L-ncb)/nac)))
       slo=max(slo,lo); shi=min(shi,hi)
       if slo>shi+1e-11: ok=False
       if ok:
          for m in (m1,m2):
             A=a[m]; C=c[m]-L; disc=A*A-4.0*C
             if disc<-1e-10: ok=False; break
             rt=np.sqrt(max(0.0,disc)); r1=(-A-rt)*0.5; r2=(-A+rt)*0.5
             slo=max(slo,r1); shi=min(shi,r2)
             if slo>shi+1e-11: ok=False; break
       if ok: Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi; slo=lo; shi=hi; ok=True
    if zmax>L+1e-9: ok=False
    if ok and pac.size: shi=min(shi,float(np.min((L-pcb)/pac)))
    if ok and nac.size: slo=max(slo,float(np.max((L-ncb)/nac)))
    slo=max(slo,lo); shi=min(shi,hi)
    if slo>shi+1e-9: return old
    for m in (m1,m2):
       A=a[m]; C=c[m]-L; disc=A*A-4.0*C
       if disc<-1e-8: return old
       rt=np.sqrt(max(0.0,disc)); r1=(-A-rt)*0.5; r2=(-A+rt)*0.5
       slo=max(slo,r1); shi=min(shi,r2)
    if slo>shi+1e-9: return old
    if slo<=0.0<=shi: s=0.0
    elif 0.0<slo: s=slo
    else: s=shi
    if abs(s)<1e-13: return old
    s=min(max(s,lo),hi)
    backup=c.copy(); c += a*s; c[m1]+=s*s; c[m2]+=s*s
    h[p]=float(h[p]+s); h[q]=float(h[q]-s)
    new=float(c.max())
    if new>old+1e-9:
       c[:]=backup; h[p]-=s; h[q]+=s; return old
    return new
```
In Python, `lin[m1]=lin[m2]=False` works? Yes assignment chained sets both? Actually `lin[m1] = lin[m2] = False` evaluates False assigned to lin[m2] then lin[m1]. Good.
`zero=~pos&~neg` bitwise precedence? `~pos & ~neg`. Good.
`c += a*s` with a length full L, c full. Good.
If s negative, cross s*s positive. Good.

`pair_polish` compress:
```
def pair_polish(h,n,max_pairs,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy(); c=compute_c(h)
    best_c=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy(); no=0
    k=min(80,n); low=np.argmax? no
    for low in ...? Need compute.
    low=np.sort(h)[:k]? argpartition better.
    low=np.argpartition(h,k-1)[:k]
    high=np.argpartition(h,n-k-1)[-k:] if k<n else np.arange(n)
    for it in range(max_pairs):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if it%25==0:
          low=np.argpartition(h,k-1)[:k]
          high=np.argpartition(h,n-k-1)[-k:] if k<n else np.arange(n)
       if rng.random()<0.15:
          p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
       else:
          p=int(low[int(rng.randint(k))]); q=int(high[int(rng.randint(k))])
       if p==q: continue
       new=pair_step(c,h,p,q,n)
       if new<best_c-1e-12: best_c=new; best_h=h.copy(); no=0
       else:
          no+=1
          if no>1200: break
    h=project_feasible(h,n); cur=float(compute_c(h).max())
    if cur<best_c: best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,2.0*best_c/n
```
If n=0? no. If n<2, `argpartition(h,k-1)` k=1 okay; pair skip. `high` if k<n? if n=1,k=1 high arange. Pair skip p==q. Return.
Computing low/high before loop for n=1 okay. `rng.random()` no.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); overall=t0+max(10.0,float(budget_s)-5.0); rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try: h=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception: h=np.full(700,0.5)
    n=int(h.size)
    if n<8: h=np.full(700,0.5); n=700
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_of(h,n)
    ph=min(overall,time.time()+150.0)
    if ph>time.time()+5.0:
       h1,c1=exact_cd(best_h,n,100,ph,rng); best_h,best_c=better_vec(...)
    ph=min(overall,time.time()+180.0)
    if ph>time.time()+5.0:
       h2,c2=pair_polish(best_h,n,2500,ph,rng); best...
    if time.time()<overall-80.0:
       ph=min(overall-20.0,time.time()+120.0)
       if ph>time.time()+5.0: h3,c3=exact_cd(best_h,n,80,ph,rng); update
    # multi
    nbase=len(best_h)
    for nt in (800,650,550,450,350):
       if time.time()>overall-80.0: break
       if nt==nbase or nt>1000: continue
       hi=interp_h(best_h,nbase,nt)
       ph=min(overall-25.0,time.time()+25.0)
       if ph>time.time()+3.0:
          ho,co=exact_cd(hi,nt,30,ph,rng); best=...
          nbase=len(best_h)
          if time.time()<overall-30.0:
             ph2=min(overall-15.0,time.time()+12.0)
             if ph2>time.time()+2.0:
                ho2,co2=pair_polish(hi,nt,300,ph2,rng); best=...
    # random
    nbase=len(best_h)
    for scale in (0.15,0.05,0.02):
       if time.time()>overall-50.0: break
       hp=project_feasible(best_h+rng.normal(0.0,scale,nbase),nbase)
       ph=min(overall-25.0,time.time()+25.0)
       if ph>time.time()+3.0:
          ho,co=exact_cd(hp,nbase,25,ph,rng); best=...
          if time.time()<overall-25.0:
             ph2=min(overall-15.0,time.time()+12.0)
             if ph2>time.time()+2.0:
                ho2,co2=pair_polish(ho,nbase,250,ph2,rng); best=...
    nbase=len(best_h)
    ph=min(overall-5.0,time.time()+35.0)
    if ph>time.time()+2.0:
       ho,co=exact_cd(best_h,nbase,80,ph,rng); best=...
    if time.time()<overall-4.0:
       ph=min(overall-2.0,time.time()+20.0)
       if ph>time.time()+1.0:
          ho,co=pair_polish(best_h,nbase,500,ph,rng); best=...
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h))
    best_c=c5_of(best_h,len(best_h))
    return best_h,best_c,int(len(best_h))
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def project_feasible(h, n):
    targ = float(n) / 2.0
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(h.sum())
    if abs(s - targ) < 1e-14:
        return h
    if s > targ:
        lo, hi = 0.0, 1.0
    else:
        lo, hi = -1.0, 0.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        x = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        sx = float(x.sum())
        if sx < targ:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    x = np.clip(h - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = targ - float(x.sum())
    if d != 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - x)) if d > 0 else int(np.argmax(x))
        x[idx] = float(np.clip(x[idx] + d, 0.0, 1.0))
    return x


def compute_c(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def c5_of(h, n):
    return float(np.max(compute_c(h)) * 2.0 / n)


def coord_info(h, p, n):
    off = n - 1
    ms = np.arange(off - p, 2 * n - 1 - p, dtype=int)
    jf = p + ms - off
    jb = p - ms + off
    a = np.zeros(len(ms))
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    if vf.any():
        a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    if vb.any():
        a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    return ms, a


def solve_1d(c, h, p, n):
    off = n - 1
    h0 = float(h[p])
    ms, a = coord_info(h, p, n)
    cb = c[ms].copy()
    di = np.where(ms == off)[0]
    has = bool(di.size)
    if has:
        a[di[0]] = 0.0
        cdiag = cb[di[0]]

    m0 = int(ms[0])
    m1 = int(ms[-1])
    last = 2 * n - 2
    unaff = 0.0
    if m0 > 0:
        unaff = float(np.max(c[:m0]))
    if m1 < last:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[m1 + 1:])))

    valid = np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool)
    if has:
        valid[di[0]] = False
    neg = valid & (a < -1e-13)
    pos = valid & (a > 1e-13)
    zero = valid & ~neg & ~pos
    pac = a[pos]
    pcb = cb[pos]
    nac = a[neg]
    ncb = cb[neg]
    zmax = float(np.max(cb[zero])) if zero.any() else -1e30

    Llo = max(0.0, unaff)
    Lhi = float(np.max(c))
    for _ in range(42):
        L = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        xlo, xhi = 0.0, 1.0
        ok = True
        if zmax > L + 1e-10:
            ok = False
        if ok and pac.size:
            xhi = min(xhi, float(np.min((L - pcb) / pac)))
        if ok and nac.size:
            xlo = max(xlo, float(np.max((L - ncb) / nac)))
        xlo = max(xlo, 0.0)
        xhi = min(xhi, 1.0)
        if xlo > xhi + 1e-11:
            ok = False
        if ok and has:
            Cq = L - cdiag + h0 - h0 * h0
            if Cq < -1e-10:
                ok = False
            elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-10:
                Cq = max(0.0, Cq)
                disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq)
                sq = np.sqrt(disc)
                r1 = (1.0 - sq) * 0.5
                r2 = (1.0 + sq) * 0.5
                if xlo > r1 + 1e-10 and xhi < r2 - 1e-10:
                    ok = False
        if ok:
            Lhi = L
        else:
            Llo = L

    L = Lhi
    xlo, xhi = 0.0, 1.0
    ok = True
    if zmax > L + 1e-9:
        ok = False
    if ok and pac.size:
        xhi = min(xhi, float(np.min((L - pcb) / pac)))
    if ok and nac.size:
        xlo = max(xlo, float(np.max((L - ncb) / nac)))
    xlo = max(xlo, 0.0)
    xhi = min(xhi, 1.0)
    if not ok or xlo > xhi + 1e-9:
        return h0

    segs = []
    if has:
        Cq = L - cdiag + h0 - h0 * h0
        if Cq < -1e-9:
            return h0
        if Cq < 0.25 - 1e-9:
            Cq = max(0.0, Cq)
            disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq)
            sq = np.sqrt(disc)
            r1 = (1.0 - sq) * 0.5
            r2 = (1.0 + sq) * 0.5
            l = max(xlo, 0.0)
            r = min(xhi, r1)
            if l <= r + 1e-9:
                segs.append((l, r))
            l = max(xlo, r2)
            r = min(xhi, 1.0)
            if l <= r + 1e-9:
                segs.append((l, r))
        else:
            segs.append((xlo, xhi))
    else:
        segs.append((xlo, xhi))

    bestx = h0
    bestd = 1e100
    for l, r in segs:
        if h0 <= l:
            d = l - h0
            x = l
        elif h0 >= r:
            d = h0 - r
            x = r
        else:
            d = 0.0
            x = h0
        if d < bestd:
            bestd = d
            bestx = x
    return float(np.clip(bestx, 0.0, 1.0))


def update_corr(c, h, p, old, neu, n):
    delta = neu - old
    if abs(delta) < 1e-14:
        return
    ms, a = coord_info(h, p, n)
    c[ms] += delta * a
    off = n - 1
    di = np.where(ms == off)[0]
    if di.size:
        c[ms[di[0]]] -= delta * delta


def exact_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    c = compute_c(h)
    best_c = 2.0 * float(c.max()) / n
    best_h = h.copy()

    for it in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if it % 2 == 0:
            order = list(np.argsort(np.abs(h - 0.5)))
        else:
            order = list(range(n))
            rng.shuffle(order)
        improved = False
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            h0 = float(h[p])
            if h0 <= 1e-12 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-12:
                continue
            xn = solve_1d(c, h, p, n)
            if abs(xn - h0) > 1e-11:
                update_corr(c, h, p, h0, xn, n)
                h[p] = xn
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        h_new = project_feasible(h, n)
        if np.max(np.abs(h_new - h)) > 1e-13:
            h = h_new
            c = compute_c(h)
        else:
            h = h_new
        cur = 2.0 * float(c.max()) / n
        if cur < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cur = 2.0 * float(compute_c(h).max()) / n
    if cur < best_c - 1e-13:
        best_c = cur
        best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def deriv_one(h, p, n):
    full = np.zeros(2 * n - 1)
    ms, a = coord_info(h, p, n)
    full[ms] = a
    return full


def pair_step(c, h, p, q, n):
    if p == q:
        return float(c.max())
    a = deriv_one(h, p, n) - deriv_one(h, q, n)
    old = float(c.max())
    if old <= 1e-14:
        return old
    lo = max(-h[p], h[q] - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - h[p], h[q])
    if hi <= lo + 1e-13:
        return old
    off = n - 1
    d = abs(p - q)
    m1 = off + d
    m2 = off - d
    cross = (m1, m2)
    lin = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=bool)
    lin[m1] = False
    lin[m2] = False
    al = a[lin]
    cl = c[lin]
    pos = al > 1e-12
    neg = al < -1e-12
    pac = al[pos]
    pcb = cl[pos]
    nac = al[neg]
    ncb = cl[neg]
    zero = ~pos & ~neg
    zmax = float(np.max(cl[zero])) if zero.any() else -1e30

    Llo = 0.0
    Lhi = old
    for _ in range(42):
        L = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        slo, shi = lo, hi
        ok = True
        if zmax > L + 1e-10:
            ok = False
        if ok and pac.size:
            shi = min(shi, float(np.min((L - pcb) / pac)))
        if ok and nac.size:
            slo = max(slo, float(np.max((L - ncb) / nac)))
        slo = max(slo, lo)
        shi = min(shi, hi)
        if slo > shi + 1e-11:
            ok = False
        if ok:
            for m in cross:
                A = a[m]
                C = c[m] - L
                disc = A * A - 4.0 * C
                if disc < -1e-10:
                    ok = False
                    break
                rt = np.sqrt(max(0.0, disc))
                r1 = (-A - rt) * 0.5
                r2 = (-A + rt) * 0.5
                slo = max(slo, r1)
                shi = min(shi, r2)
                if slo > shi + 1e-11:
                    ok = False
                    break
        if ok:
            Lhi = L
        else:
            Llo = L

    L = Lhi
    slo, shi = lo, hi
    ok = True
    if zmax > L + 1e-9:
        ok = False
    if ok and pac.size:
        shi = min(shi, float(np.min((L - pcb) / pac)))
    if ok and nac.size:
        slo = max(slo, float(np.max((L - ncb) / nac)))
    slo = max(slo, lo)
    shi = min(shi, hi)
    if slo > shi + 1e-9:
        return old
    for m in cross:
        A = a[m]
        C = c[m] - L
        disc = A * A - 4.0 * C
        if disc < -1e-8:
            return old
        rt = np.sqrt(max(0.0, disc))
        r1 = (-A - rt) * 0.5
        r2 = (-A + rt) * 0.5
        slo = max(slo, r1)
        shi = min(shi, r2)
    if slo > shi + 1e-9:
        return old

    if slo <= 0.0 <= shi:
        s = 0.0
    elif 0.0 < slo:
        s = slo
    else:
        s = shi
    if abs(s) < 1e-13:
        return old
    s = min(max(s, lo), hi)

    backup = c.copy()
    c += a * s
    c[m1] += s * s
    c[m2] += s * s
    h[p] = float(h[p] + s)
    h[q] = float(h[q] - s)
    new = float(c.max())
    if new > old + 1e-9:
        c[:] = backup
        h[p] -= s
        h[q] += s
        return old
    return new


def pair_polish(h, n, max_pairs, deadline, rng):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    c = compute_c(h)
    best_c = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    no = 0
    k = min(80, n)
    low = np.argpartition(h, k - 1)[:k]
    high = np.argpartition(h, n - k - 1)[-k:] if k < n else np.arange(n)

    for it in range(max_pairs):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if it % 25 == 0:
            low = np.argpartition(h, k - 1)[:k]
            high = np.argpartition(h, n - k - 1)[-k:] if k < n else np.arange(n)
        if rng.random() < 0.15:
            p = int(rng.randint(n))
            q = int(rng.randint(n))
        else:
            p = int(low[int(rng.randint(k))])
            q = int(high[int(rng.randint(k))])
        if p == q:
            continue
        new = pair_step(c, h, p, q, n)
        if new < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = new
            best_h = h.copy()
            no = 0
        else:
            no += 1
            if no > 1200:
                break

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cur = float(compute_c(h).max())
    if cur < best_c:
        best_c = cur
        best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, 2.0 * best_c / n


def interp_h(h, n0, n1):
    if n0 == n1:
        return h.copy()
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0, endpoint=False)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n1, endpoint=False)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, h), n1)


def better_vec(bh, bc, h, c):
    if c < bc - 1e-12:
        return h, c
    return bh, bc


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    overall = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
        h = np.full(700, 0.5)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n < 8:
        h = np.full(700, 0.5)
        n = 700

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_of(h, n)

    ph = min(overall, time.time() + 150.0)
    if ph > time.time() + 5.0:
        best_h, best_c = better_vec(best_h, best_c, *exact_cd(best_h, len(best_h), 100, ph, rng))

    ph = min(overall, time.time() + 180.0)
    if ph > time.time() + 5.0:
        best_h, best_c = better_vec(best_h, best_c, *pair_polish(best_h, len(best_h), 2500, ph, rng))

    if time.time() < overall - 80.0:
        ph = min(overall - 20.0, time.time() + 120.0)
        if ph > time.time() + 5.0:
            best_h, best_c = better_vec(best_h, best_c, *exact_cd(best_h, len(best_h), 80, ph, rng))

    nbase = len(best_h)
    for nt in (800, 650, 550, 450, 350):
        if time.time() > overall - 80.0:
            break
        if nt == nbase or nt > 1000:
            continue
        hi = interp_h(best_h, nbase, nt)
        ph = min(overall - 25.0, time.time() + 25.0)
        if ph > time.time() + 3.0:
            best_h, best_c = better_vec(best_h, best_c, *exact_cd(hi, nt, 30, ph, rng))
            nbase = len(best_h)
            if time.time() < overall - 30.0:
                ph2 = min(overall - 15.0, time.time() + 12.0)
                if ph2 > time.time() + 2.0:
                    best_h, best_c = better_vec(best_h, best_c, *pair_polish(hi, nt, 300, ph2, rng))
                    nbase = len(best_h)

    nbase = len(best_h)
    for scale in (0.15, 0.05, 0.02):
        if time.time() > overall - 50.0:
            break
        hp = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, nbase), nbase)
        ph = min(overall - 25.0, time.time() + 25.0)
        if ph > time.time() + 3.0:
            best_h, best_c = better_vec(best_h, best_c, *exact_cd(hp, nbase, 25, ph, rng))
            if time.time() < overall - 25.0:
                ph2 = min(overall - 15.0, time.time() + 12.0)
                if ph2 > time.time() + 2.0:
                    best_h, best_c = better_vec(best_h, best_c, *pair_polish(hp, nbase, 250, ph2, rng))

    nbase = len(best_h)
    ph = min(overall - 5.0, time.time() + 35.0)
    if ph > time.time() + 2.0:
        best_h, best_c = better_vec(best_h, best_c, *exact_cd(best_h, nbase, 80, ph, rng))
    if time.time() < overall - 4.0:
        ph = min(overall - 2.0, time.time() + 20.0)
        if ph > time.time() + 1.0:
            best_h, best_c = better_vec(best_h, best_c, *pair_polish(best_h, len(best_h), 500, ph, rng))

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = c5_of(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, int(len(best_h))
```
